## Full File Analysis Report

---

### 1. `usePets.ts` — Fetch URLs & Error Handling

**`API_BASE` is resolved at module load time** (line 15): `const API_BASE = getApiBase();`

The hook makes calls to **five distinct endpoints**, all prefixed with `/api/pets`:

| Function | Method | URL |
|---|---|---|
| `fetchPets` | GET | `${API_BASE}/api/pets?user_id=${userId}` |
| `fetchActivePet` | GET | `${API_BASE}/api/pets/active/current?user_id=${userId}` |
| `unlockPet` | POST | `${API_BASE}/api/pets/${petId}/unlock?user_id=${userId}` |
| `setActivePet` | PUT | `${API_BASE}/api/pets/active?user_id=${userId}` |
| `clearActivePet` | DELETE | `${API_BASE}/api/pets/active?user_id=${userId}` |

**Error Handling pattern:**
- `fetchPets`: uses a `try/catch` block; on `!response.ok` throws `'Failed to fetch pets'`; the catch sets `error` state via `setError(message)` and logs `console.error`. Uses `finally` to always clear `isLoading`.
- `fetchActivePet`: specially handles `response.status === 404` (no active pet) gracefully returning `null` — no error thrown. Other failures go to catch → `console.error`.
- `unlockPet` / `setActivePet` / `clearActivePet`: catch-and-return pattern — errors are caught and returned as `{ success: false, message }` rather than setting component error state.

---

### 2. `config.ts` — What `getApiBase()` Returns

**Priority order:**

1. **`VITE_API_BASE` env var** (set via `.env` or Vercel): Used as-is. If it lacks `http://` or `https://`, it auto-prepends `https://`.
2. **Fallback (no env var set):** Returns the hardcoded string `'http://localhost:8000'`

`getWsUrl()` follows the same pattern but for WebSocket URLs (`VITE_WS_URL` → derive from `getApiBase()` by replacing `https://` → `wss://` / `http://` → `ws://` → fallback `'ws://localhost:8000'`).

So in local dev without a `.env`: **`getApiBase()` = `'http://localhost:8000'`**

---

### 3. `backend/api/pets.py` — Route Prefix & Endpoints

**The router itself has no prefix** — `router = APIRouter()` on line 25 with no `prefix=` argument. The prefix is applied wherever this router is `include_router`'d in the main app (not visible in this file).

**However**, all route decorators are defined as `/pets/...`, so the final URL depends on the main app mount. Given the frontend calls `/api/pets` and the Vite proxy strips `/api` (see below), the backend effectively serves these at:

| Decorator | Method | Full Path (as mounted) |
|---|---|---|
| `@router.get("/pets")` | GET | List all pets with user unlock status |
| `@router.get("/pets/{pet_id}")` | GET | Get single pet by ID |
| `@router.post("/pets/{pet_id}/unlock")` | POST | Unlock a pet for user |
| `@router.put("/pets/active")` | PUT | Set user's active pet |
| `@router.get("/pets/active/current")` | GET | Get current active pet |
| `@router.delete("/pets/active")` | DELETE | Clear active pet |
| `@router.post("/pets/admin/create")` | POST | [ADMIN] Create new pet |
| `@router.put("/pets/admin/{pet_id}")` | PUT | [ADMIN] Update pet |
| `@router.delete("/pets/admin/{pet_id}")` | DELETE | [ADMIN] Soft-delete pet |

**Important note — potential routing conflict:** FastAPI processes routes in registration order. `PUT /pets/active` and `GET /pets/active/current` are defined **before** `GET /pets/{pet_id}`, which is correct. However, `DELETE /pets/active` (line 330) comes **after** `DELETE /pets/admin/{pet_id}` — this specific ordering should be fine since the path segments differ, but worth being aware of.

---

### 4. `vite.config.ts` — Build Settings

**Manual Chunk Splitting (only 2 named chunks defined):**
| Chunk Name | Included modules |
|---|---|
| `three-vendor` | `node_modules/three`, `node_modules/@react-three` |
| `react-vendor` | `node_modules/react`, `node_modules/react-dom`, `node_modules/react-router` |

Everything else goes into the default Rollup chunks. **No `minify` override is set** — Vite defaults apply (esbuild minification in production). **No `sourcemap` setting** — defaults to `false` in production.

**Dev Server settings of note:**
- Host: `0.0.0.0`, Port: `5173`
- HMR `clientPort: 443` (designed for tunnel/HTTPS proxy environments like Cloudflare, ngrok)
- CORS fully open (`*`)

**Proxy rules (dev only):**
- `/api/*` → `http://localhost:8000` with **`rewrite: path.replace(/^\/api/, '')`** — this strips the `/api` prefix before hitting the backend. This means the backend receives `/pets` not `/api/pets`.
- `/assets/model2D` → `http://localhost:8000`
- `/ws` → `ws://localhost:8000` (WebSocket)

**This is a critical detail for the fetch URL question** — in dev, `usePets` calls `http://localhost:8000/api/pets?user_id=...` (since `API_BASE = localhost:8000`), which hits the Vite proxy at `/api/pets`, strips to `/pets`, then hits the backend. In production, the `VITE_API_BASE` must point directly at the backend host (no proxy), so the backend router must be mounted at `/api` in the FastAPI main app.

**Asset types included in build:** `.iset`, `.fset`, `.fset3`, `.mind`, `.flist` (AR marker/mind file formats).

---

- **StrictMode is deliberately disabled** — commented out with a note: *"causes double-mount which disrupts iframe camera initialization"* (AR camera bug workaround). This is a `TODO` to re-enable after AR stabilizes.
- App is wrapped in `BrowserRouter` (client-side routing).
- Imports: `global.css` and `index.css` both loaded.
- Standard `createRoot` / `render` — no providers (Redux, Context, etc.) at root level; those are presumably inside `<App />`.

## Assistant (Compaction · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 86.5s)

## Goal

Fix three issues in the EduAR platform (Vietnamese primary school AR English learning app, ages 6–12) and push to production (Vercel frontend + Render backend):

1. **PiP scanner UI → background silent scan**: Remove the visible 120px circular PiP overlay in the top-left during VIEWING mode. Keep the `ar-scanner.html` iframe alive/running silently in the background (invisible, full-size, behind the AR viewer) so second-card QR detection still works without any UI clutter.
2. **Pets API broken**: `usePets.ts` calls `/api/pets` but the backend mounts `pet_router` at `API_V1_PREFIX = "/api/v1"`, making the real endpoint `/api/v1/pets`. All 5 fetch URLs in `usePets.ts` need to change from `/api/pets` to `/api/v1/pets`.
3. **Performance**: LCP 9.88s, INP 576ms, TTFB 0.88s — terrible scores on free Render + Vercel tiers. Multiple targeted optimizations needed.

---

## Instructions

### Stack & Architecture
- **Backend**: FastAPI + MongoDB (Beanie ODM). `API_V1_PREFIX = "/api/v1"` (confirmed in `backend/settings.py` line 33). Hosted on **Render free tier** (sleeps after 15 min inactivity).
- **Frontend**: React/TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind v4. Hosted on **Vercel free tier**.
- **3D**: Three.js + `@react-three/fiber` + `@react-three/drei`
- `USER_ID = 'demo-user'` hardcoded everywhere (no real auth)
- `API_BASE = getApiBase()` from `frontend-web/src/config.ts` → returns `VITE_API_BASE` env var or falls back to `http://localhost:8000`
- All pets endpoints: `GET/POST/PUT/DELETE /api/v1/pets/...` (NOT `/api/pets`)
- Health check endpoint: `GET /health` (no prefix, at root)

### Design System — Claymorphism (already implemented)
- CSS tokens in `index.css`: `--color-primary: #5B8DEF`, `--color-secondary: #7BC67E`, `--color-accent: #FFB347`, `--color-bg: #F8FAFF`, etc.
- Z-index scale: `--z-chatbot: 150`, `--z-nav: 200`, `--z-modal: 300`, `--z-ar: 400`
- Utility classes: `.clay-card`, `.clay-btn`, `.clay-btn-primary`, `.clay-btn-accent`, `.clay-btn-secondary`

### Key Constraints
- Must stay within **free tier** for both Render and Vercel — no infrastructure changes
- User is on **iPhone iOS 18.7 Safari** — iOS camera access in iframes is sensitive
- The background PiP scanner already works (confirmed: `tree_palm_02` was detected while in VIEWING mode in the mobile debug log)
- Do NOT break the `fromPiP` detection logic in `ARContainerV2.tsx` message handler

---

## Discoveries

### Root Cause: Pets API Broken
- `usePets.ts` calls `${API_BASE}/api/pets` (5 occurrences: fetchPets, fetchActivePet, unlockPet, setActivePet, clearActivePet)
- `main.py` line 188–192: `app.include_router(pet_router, prefix=settings.API_V1_PREFIX, tags=["Pets"])`
- `settings.py` line 33: `API_V1_PREFIX: str = "/api/v1"`
- Therefore actual backend URL is `/api/v1/pets/...` not `/api/pets/...`
- The `{}` error in mobile debug is because `Error` objects serialize to `{}` in JSON — the hook gets a 404, throws `new Error('Failed to fetch pets')`, and the debug panel shows `{}`
- **Fix**: Replace all 5 occurrences of `${API_BASE}/api/pets` → `${API_BASE}/api/v1/pets` in `usePets.ts`
- The session notes from before this conversation said "Pets API uses `/api/pets` (NOT `/api/v1/pets`)" — THIS WAS WRONG. Code confirms it IS `/api/v1/pets`.

### Root Cause: Performance
- **209 `console.log` statements** across frontend `src/` — serialized and sent to mobile Safari inspector on every AR frame. This is extremely expensive on iOS.
- **Render free tier cold start**: 70+ second delay observed (18:08:55 QR scan → 18:10:08 quiz data loaded = 73s). Nothing can fix this except pre-warming.
- **LCP 9.88s**: SPA with no SSR — all content gated behind API responses. Page shell is fast but "largest element" only paints after fetch resolves.
- **TTFB 0.88s**: Vercel free tier serving from single region.
- **Vite currently uses esbuild minification** (default) — does NOT strip console.log. Adding Terser with `drop_console: true` would eliminate all 209 log calls from production bundle.
- **Top console.log offenders**: `Nft.tsx` (22), `MultiFlashcardTracker.ts` (13), `RuntimeBridge.ts` (13), `DetectQrService.ts` (11), `LearnAR.tsx` (11)

### Background Scanner Architecture (already implemented in ARContainerV2.tsx)
- The `pipRef` iframe loads `ar-scanner.html` and stays alive during VIEWING phase
- The `window.addEventListener('message', handleMessage)` handler already distinguishes `fromPiP` vs main iframe via `event.source === pipRef.current?.contentWindow`
- During VIEWING: `QR_DETECTED` from PiP → only calls `onQRDetected()`, does NOT call `transitionTo('LOADING')` → parent hook adds second card naturally
- The visual PiP (120px circle, expand/collapse, dismiss, "Scan more" button) uses `showPiP` + `pipExpanded` state — user wants these REMOVED entirely
- **Safe hidden iframe approach**: Set PiP iframe to `opacity: 0; position: absolute; width: 100%; height: 100%; top: 0; left: 0; z-index: 0; pointer-events: none;` — keeps it in viewport (browser won't suppress camera) but fully invisible, behind main viewer at `z-index: 1`
- iOS Safari confirmed working with this dual-iframe camera approach (tested on iPhone iOS 18.7)

### Vite Proxy (Dev only)
- Dev proxy: `/api/*` → `http://localhost:8000` with `rewrite: path.replace(/^\/api/, '')` — strips `/api` prefix
- In production, `API_BASE` points directly to Render, so full URL is used as-is
- This proxy causes confusion: in dev, `/api/v1/quiz/...` → proxy strips `/api` → backend gets `/v1/quiz/...`... actually in dev the fetches use full `http://localhost:8000/api/v1/...` URLs (not relative), so they bypass the proxy entirely

134:     // ========== SEND TYPED MESSAGE (New Protocol) ==========
135:     const sendTypedMessage = useCallback(<K extends ARMessageType>(

137:         payload: K extends keyof ARMessagePayloadMap ? ARMessagePayloadMap[K] : unknown
138:     ) => {
139:         if (!iframeRef.current?.contentWindow) {

151:

158:             const { type, payload } = msg as ARMessage;
159: 
160:             // Determine which iframe sent this

172:

193:                     break;

195: 
196:                 // ── Scanner error ──

<content>1: // src/hooks/useArData.ts
2: 
3: import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
4: import type { ARTarget, ARCombo } from '../types';
5: import { getApiBase } from '../config';
6: import { eventBus } from '@/runtime/EventBus';
7: 
8: const API_BASE = getApiBase();
9: 
10: interface Flashcard {
11:   _id: string;
12:   qr_id: string;
13:   word: string;
14:   translation: Record<string, string>;
15:   category: string;
16:   image_url: string;
17:   audio_url: string;
18:   ar_tag: string;
19:   difficulty: string;
20:   created_at: string;
21:   image_animation_type?: string | null;
22: }
23: 
24: interface ArData {
25:   flashcard: Flashcard;
26:   targets: ARTarget[];
27:   combo: ARCombo | null;
28: }
29: 
30: export const useArData = (qrId: string | null) => {
31:   const [arData, setArData] = useState<ArData | null>(null);
32:   const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
33:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
34: 
35:   useEffect(() => {
36:     if (!qrId) {
37:       console.log('[useArData] No QR ID provided');
38:       return;
39:     }
40: 
41:     const fetchData = async () => {
42:       console.log('[useArData] Fetching data for QR ID:', qrId);
43:       setIsLoading(true);
44:       setError(null);
45: 
46:       try {
47:         const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/v1/flashcard/${qrId}`);
48: 
49:         if (!response.ok) {
50:           throw new Error(`Failed to fetch AR data: ${response.statusText}`);
51:         }
52: 
53:         const data = await response.json();
54: 
55:         if (!data.flashcard || !data.target) {
56:           throw new Error('Invalid or empty data received from API');
57:         }
58: 
59:         // Helper function to build full URL for backend assets
60:         const buildUrl = (path: string | undefined): string | undefined => {
61:           if (!path) return undefined;
62:           if (path.startsWith('http://') || path.startsWith('https://')) {
63:             return path;
64:           }
65:           const cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
66:           return `${API_BASE}${cleanPath}`;
67:         };
68: 
69:         // Helper to get frontend origin for static assets like NFT
70:         const getFrontendOrigin = (): string => {
71:           if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
72:             return window.location.origin;
73:           }
74:           return '';
75:         };
76: 
77:         // Transform target data
78:         const target: ARTarget = {
79:           tag: data.target.ar_tag,
80:           nft_base_url: (() => {
81:             const rawPath = data.target.nft_base_url;
82:             if (rawPath.startsWith('http://') || rawPath.startsWith('https://')) {
83:               return rawPath;
84:             }
85:             const cleanPath = rawPath
86:               .replace(/^\/public\//, '')
87:               .replace(/^public\//, '')
88:               .replace(/^\//, '');
89: 
90:             const frontendOrigin = getFrontendOrigin();
91:             return frontendOrigin ? `${frontendOrigin}/${cleanPath}` : `/${cleanPath}`;
92:           })(),
93:           image_2d_url: buildUrl(data.target.image_2d_url),
94:           model_3d_url: buildUrl(data.target.model_3d_url) || '',
95:           position: data.target.position,
96:           rotation: data.target.rotation,
97:           scale: data.target.scale,
98:         };
99: 
100:         // Transform combo data
101:         const rawCombo = data.related_combos?.length > 0 ? data.related_combos[0] : null;
102:         const combo = rawCombo ? {
103:           ...rawCombo,
104:           image_2d_url: buildUrl(rawCombo.image_2d_url),
105:           model_3d_url: buildUrl(rawCombo.model_3d_url),
106:         } : null;
107: 
108:         const transformedData: ArData = {
109:           flashcard: data.flashcard,
110:           targets: [target],
111:           combo: combo,
112:         };
113: 
114:         console.log('✅ [useArData] Transformed AR data:', transformedData);
115:         setArData(transformedData);
116: 
117:         // 🚀 SUCCESS EVENT EMISSION
118:         // This decouples the AR injection logic from React's state/render cycle
119:         eventBus.emit('AR_DATA_LOADED' as any, transformedData);
120: 
121:       } catch (err) {
122:         const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error occurred';
123:         console.error('❌ [useArData] error:', errorMessage);
124:         setError(errorMessage);
125:         setArData(null);
126:       } finally {
127:         setIsLoading(false);
128:       }
129:     };
130: 
131:     fetchData();
132:   }, [qrId]);
133: 
134:   return { arData, isLoading, error };
135: };

(End of file - total 135 lines)
</content>
```

**Task 6: Add localStorage caching to the three data hooks.**

The strategy: stale-while-revalidate with 10-minute TTL. Check cache → return instantly if fresh → always fetch + update cache in background (or on miss).

I'll add a small inline cache utility at the top of each hook (no shared file needed to keep it self-contained):

**Tool: edit**

**Input:**
```json
{
  "filePath": "E:\\Daniel Project\\Graduted Project\\Edu-platform\\frontend-web\\src\\hooks\\useQuizData.ts",
  "oldString": "// src/hooks/useQuizData.ts\n\nimport { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport type { QuizSessionData } from '../types';\nimport { getApiBase } from '../config';\n\nconst API_BASE = getApiBase();\n\n/**\n * Hook to fetch quiz questions from backend\n */\nexport function useQuizData(qrId: string | null) {\n  const [quizData, setQuizData] = useState<QuizSessionData | null>(null);\n  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!qrId) {\n      setQuizData(null);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const fetchQuizData = async () => {\n      console.log('📚 [useQuizData] Fetching quiz for QR:', qrId);\n      setIsLoading(true);\n      setError(null);\n\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/v1/quiz/${qrId}`);\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          if (response.status === 404) {\n            throw new Error('No quiz available for this flashcard');\n          }\n          throw new Error(`Failed to fetch quiz: ${response.statusText}`);\n        }\n\n        const data: QuizSessionData = await response.json();\n        console.log('✅ [useQuizData] Quiz loaded:', data);\n        setQuizData(data);\n      } catch (err) {\n        const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error';\n        console.error('❌ [useQuizData] Error:', errorMessage);\n        setError(errorMessage);\n        setQuizData(null);\n      } finally {\n        setIsLoading(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    fetchQuizData();\n  }, [qrId]);\n\n  return { quizData, isLoading, error };\n}",
  "newString": "// src/hooks/useQuizData.ts\n\nimport { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport type { QuizSessionData } from '../types';\nimport { getApiBase } from '../config';\n\nconst API_BASE = getApiBase();\n\n// ── localStorage cache helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────\nconst CACHE_TTL = 10 * 60 * 1000; // 10 minutes\n\nfunction getCached<T>(key: string): T | null {\n  try {\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    const { data, ts } = JSON.parse(raw) as { data: T; ts: number };\n    if (Date.now() - ts > CACHE_TTL) { localStorage.removeItem(key); return null; }\n    return data;\n  } catch { return null; }\n}\n\nfunction setCache(key: string, data: unknown): void {\n  try { localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({ data, ts: Date.now() })); } catch { /* quota exceeded — ignore */ }\n}\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * Hook to fetch quiz questions from backend.\n * Serves cached data instantly (<10 min TTL), then revalidates in background.\n */\nexport function useQuizData(qrId: string | null) {\n  const [quizData, setQuizData] = useState<QuizSessionData | null>(null);\n  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!qrId) {\n      setQuizData(null);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const cacheKey = `quiz:${qrId}`;\n\n    const fetchQuizData = async (showLoading: boolean) => {\n      if (showLoading) { setIsLoading(true); setError(null); }\n\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/v1/quiz/${qrId}`);\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          if (response.status === 404) {\n            throw new Error('No quiz available for this flashcard');\n          }\n          throw new Error(`Failed to fetch quiz: ${response.statusText}`);\n        }\n\n        const data: QuizSessionData = await response.json();\n        setCache(cacheKey, data);\n        setQuizData(data);\n      } catch (err) {\n        const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error';\n        // Only surface the error when there's no cached data to show\n        if (!quizData) setError(errorMessage);\n      } finally {\n        if (showLoading) setIsLoading(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    // Serve cache immediately; skip the loading spinner if we have fresh data\n    const cached = getCached<QuizSessionData>(cacheKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      setQuizData(cached);\n      // Revalidate silently in background (no loading indicator)\n      fetchQuizData(false);\n    } else {\n      fetchQuizData(true);\n    }\n  }, [qrId]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n\n  return { quizData, isLoading, error };\n}"
}

''}:${gameType ?? ''}`;\n\n    const fetchGameData = async (showLoading: boolean) => {\n      if (showLoading) { setIsLoading(true); setError(null); }\n\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(url);\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          if (response.status === 404) {\n            throw new Error('No games available for this selection');\n          }\n          throw new Error(`Failed to fetch game: ${response.statusText}`);\n        }\n\n        const data: GameSessionData = await response.json();\n        setCache(cacheKey, data);\n        setGameData(data);\n      } catch (err) {\n        const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error';\n        if (!gameData) setError(errorMessage);\n      } finally {\n        if (showLoading) setIsLoading(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const cached = getCached<GameSessionData>(cacheKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      setGameData(cached);\n      fetchGameData(false);\n    } else {\n      fetchGameData(true);\n    }\n  }, [qrId, difficulty, gameType]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n\n  return { gameData, isLoading, error };\n}",
  "oldString": "// src/hooks/useGameData.ts\n\nimport { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport type { GameSessionData, GameDifficulty, GameType } from '../types';\nimport { getApiBase } from '../config';\n\nconst API_BASE = getApiBase();\n\n/**\n * Hook to fetch game challenges from backend with game type filter\n */\nexport function useGameData(\n  qrId: string | null,\n  difficulty: GameDifficulty | null = null,\n  gameType: GameType | null = null\n) {\n  const [gameData, setGameData] = useState<GameSessionData | null>(null);\n  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!qrId) {\n      setGameData(null);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const fetchGameData = async () => {\n      console.log('🎮 [useGameData] Fetching:', { qrId, difficulty, gameType });\n      setIsLoading(true);\n      setError(null);\n\n      try {\n        // Build query params\n        const params = new URLSearchParams();\n        if (difficulty) params.append('difficulty', difficulty);\n        if (gameType) params.append('game_type', gameType);\n\n        const queryString = params.toString();\n        const url = `${API_BASE}/api/v1/game/${qrId}${queryString ? `?${queryString}` : ''}`;\n\n        console.log('📡 [useGameData] URL:', url);\n        const response = await fetch(url);\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          if (response.status === 404) {\n            throw new Error('No games available for this selection');\n          }\n          throw new Error(`Failed to fetch game: ${response.statusText}`);\n        }\n\n        const data: GameSessionData = await response.json();\n        console.log('✅ [useGameData] Game loaded:', data);\n        setGameData(data);\n      } catch (err) {\n        const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? : null;\n      const combo = rawCombo ? {\n        ...rawCombo,\n        image_2d_url: buildUrl(rawCombo.image_2d_url),\n        model_3d_url: buildUrl(rawCombo.model_3d_url),\n      } : null;\n\n      return { flashcard: data.flashcard, targets: [target], combo };\n    };\n\n    const fetchData = async (showLoading: boolean) => {\n      if (showLoading) { setIsLoading(true); setError(null); }\n\n path : `/${path}`;\n          return `${API_BASE}${cleanPath}`;\n        };\n\n        // Helper to get frontend origin for static assets like NFT\n        const getFrontendOrigin = (): string => {\n          if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {\n            return window.location.origin;\n          }\n          return '';\n        };\n\n        // Transform target data\n        const target: ARTarget = {\n          tag: data.target.ar_tag,\n          nft_base_url: (() => {\n            const rawPath = data.target.nft_base_url;\n            if (rawPath.startsWith('http://') || rawPath.startsWith('https://')) {\n              return rawPath;\n            }\n            const cleanPath = rawPath\n              .replace(/^\\/public\\//, '')\n              .replace(/^public\\//, '')\n              .replace(/^\\//, '');\n\n            const frontendOrigin = getFrontendOrigin();\n            return frontendOrigin ? `${frontendOrigin}/${cleanPath}` : `/${cleanPath}`;\n          })(),\n          image_2d_url: buildUrl(data.target.image_2d_url),\n          model_3d_url: buildUrl(data.target.model_3d_url) || '',\n          position: data.target.position,\n          rotation: data.target.rotation,\n          scale: data.target.scale,\n        };\n\n        // Transform combo data\n        const rawCombo = data.related_combos?.length > 0 ? data.related_combos[0] : null;\n        const combo = rawCombo ? {\n          ...rawCombo,\n          image_2d_url: buildUrl(rawCombo.image_2d_url),\n          model_3d_url: buildUrl(rawCombo.model_3d_url),\n        } : null;\n\n        const transformedData: ArData = {\n          flashcard: data.flashcard,\n          targets: [target],\n          combo: combo,\n        };\n\n        console.log('✅ [useArData] Transformed AR data:', transformedData);\n        setArData(transformedData);\n\n        // 🚀 SUCCESS EVENT EMISSION\n        // This decouples the AR injection logic from React's state/render cycle\n        eventBus.emit('AR_DATA_LOADED' as any, transformedData);\n\n      } catch (err) {\n        const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error occurred';\n        console.error('❌ [useArData] error:', errorMessage);\n        setError(errorMessage);\n        setArData(null);\n      } finally {\n        setIsLoading(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    fetchData();\n  }, [qrId]);\n\n  return { arData, isLoading, error };\n};"
}
```

## Assistant (Build · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 12.8s)

All done. Pushed as `cc5c991`. Here's a summary of everything that landed:

---

## What was done (commit `cc5c991`)

### Bug fixes
| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `usePets.ts` | Fixed 5 fetch URLs: `/api/pets` → `/api/v1/pets` — was causing 404s that serialized as `{}` in the debug panel |
| `App.tsx` line 109 | Fixed 6th broken URL in `GlobalPetUnlockNotifier` (same `/api/pets` → `/api/v1/pets`) |

### AR scanner UI
| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `ARContainerV2.tsx` | Removed all visible PiP UI (120px circle, expand/collapse, dismiss, "Scan more" button, `showPiP`/`pipExpanded` state). Replaced with a single invisible full-size iframe (`opacity:0`, `zIndex:0`, `pointerEvents:none`) — camera stays alive for second-card detection, zero visual clutter |

### Performance
| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `App.tsx` | Added warmup `useEffect` that fires `GET /health` on app load to wake the Render free-tier before users hit data-fetching pages |
| `vite.config.ts` | Switched minifier to `terser` with `drop_console + drop_debugger` — strips ~209 `console.log` calls from the production bundle |
| `index.html` | Added `<link rel="preconnect">` + `<link rel="dns-prefetch">` for the Render API host |
| `useQuizData.ts` | 10-minute TTL `localStorage` stale-while-revalidate cache — repeat scans of the same QR code are instant |
| `useGameData.ts` | Same cache pattern, keyed by `qrId + difficulty + gameType` |
| `useArData.ts` | Same cache pattern; also emits `AR_DATA_LOADED` immediately from cache so AR injection doesn't wait for the network on repeat visits |

## Assistant (Compaction · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 69.5s)

---

## Goal

Fix a black screen bug on `/learn-ar` that appeared after our latest commits (`cc5c991` + `c1e85d0`) deployed to Vercel. Before those commits the AR scanner worked (camera appeared, `SCANNER_READY` fired). After deployment the screen is completely black with zero hook activity — only the mobile debug panel loads.

Secondary ongoing goals (already completed):
1. ✅ Fix pets API URL (`/api/pets` → `/api/v1/pets`)
2. ✅ Remove visible PiP scanner UI → silent background iframe
3. ✅ Performance: terser drop_console, preconnect hints, warmup ping, localStorage caching

---

## Instructions

- **Stack**: FastAPI + MongoDB backend (Render free tier), React/TypeScript/Vite frontend (Vercel free tier)
- **Mobile target**: iPhone iOS 18.7 Safari — iOS camera access in iframes is sensitive
- **No infrastructure changes** — must stay within free tier
- `USER_ID = 'demo-user'` hardcoded everywhere
- `API_BASE = getApiBase()` from `frontend-web/src/config.ts`
- All pets endpoints: `/api/v1/pets/...` (NOT `/api/pets`)
- Design system: claymorphism tokens in `index.css`, z-index scale: `--z-ar: 400`
- Z-index in ARContainerV2: main iframe = 1, background scanner iframe = 0, children = 100
- **Do not break** the `fromPiP` second-card QR detection logic in `ARContainerV2.tsx`
- Background scanner iframe must stay alive during VIEWING (iOS won't give camera to hidden iframes — use `opacity:0` not `display:none`)

---

## Discoveries

### Root cause of black screen (actively being diagnosed)

Two debug sessions from the mobile log:

**Session 1 (18:08 — OLD build, pre-deployment):**
- Works correctly: `SCANNER_READY` fires, QR detected, quiz/game data loads in ~70s (Render cold start)
- Has `console.log` output → confirms this is the PRE-terser build
- `[usePets] Fetch error: {}` at 18:10:05 — this is the OLD `/api/pets` 404 issue (now fixed)

**Session 2 (18:40 — NEW build, post-deployment):**
- Completely black screen
- Only 3 log lines (debug panel init) then nothing
- No `SCANNER_READY`, no hook logs, no iframe activity
- `drop_console:true` explains absence of console logs — but complete blackness is wrong

### What changed between sessions (commits `cc5c991` + `c1e85d0`):

**Suspect changes (in order of likelihood):**

1. **`ARContainerV2.tsx`** — Removed `showPiP`/`pipExpanded` state + `MAX_CARDS` constant + replaced visible PiP with hidden iframe. Also added empty no-op `if (newPhase === 'VIEWING') {}` block in `transitionTo`. **Most likely culprit** — structural render changes.

2. **`useArData.ts`** — Significant refactor: extracted `transformRaw` helper, added `getCached`/`setCache` localStorage functions, changed flow to stale-while-revalidate. If localStorage throws (iOS Safari private mode), `getCached` has try-catch so shouldn't crash.

3. **`vite.config.ts`** — Added `minify: 'terser'` with `drop_console: true` + `drop_debugger: true`. Could terser corrupt something in the bundle? TypeScript passed but terser runs at bundle time.

4. **`useQuizData.ts` / `useGameData.ts`** — Same localStorage caching pattern added.

5. **`App.tsx`** — Added `useEffect` warmup ping to `/health`.

### Key diagnostic clue:
The debug panel script (`/static/ar-assets/js/mobile-debug.js`) is in `index.html` as a plain `<script>` tag — completely outside React. It loads independently. The fact that it works but React produces nothing means **React is either not mounting at all, OR `LearnARV2` is rendering but the `ARContainerV2` is not displaying**.

### What the working flow looks like:
- `LearnARV2` renders immediately → mounts `ARContainerV2` with `initialPhase='SCANNING'`  
- `ARContainerV2` computes `mainSrc = '/ar-scanner.html'` for SCANNING phase
- Main iframe renders with `zIndex:1`, `position:absolute`, `inset:0`
- `ar-scanner.html` posts `SCANNER_READY` → `handleMessage` sets `isReady=true`
- Camera appears

### Potential specific bugs to check:

1. **Empty `if` block** in `transitionTo` — terser might handle this oddly (very unlikely)
2. **`LearnARV2.tsx`** might have a conditional that hides `ARContainerV2` while waiting for data — if `useArData` / `useQuizData` never resolve (or take much longer), the container might not render
3. **The `sendTypedMessage` `useCallback`** has `[isReady]` dependency — if `isReady` never becomes true (because `SCANNER_READY` never fires), something else could cascade
4. **iOS Safari + terser** — some ES2020+ syntax that terser outputs might not be supported on iOS 18.7 Safari, though this seems unlikely

---

## Accomplished

### ✅ Completed & Pushed

**Commit `cc5c991`** — "fix: pets API /api/v1/pets, silent background scanner, perf optimizations"
- `usePets.ts`: fixed 5 fetch URLs `/api/pets` → `/api/v1/pets`
- `App.tsx`: fixed `GlobalPetUnlockNotifier` URL (6th occurrence) + added Render warmup ping `useEffect`
- `ARContainerV2.tsx`: replaced visible 120px PiP circle + controls with invisible full-size background iframe (`opacity:0`, `zIndex:0`, `pointerEvents:none`)
- `vite.config.ts`: added terser minifier with `drop_console:true` + `drop_debugger:true`
- `index.html`: added `preconnect` + `dns-prefetch` for `edu-platform-api-do20.onrender.com`
- `useQuizData.ts`: added 10-min TTL localStorage stale-while-revalidate cache
- `useGameData.ts`: same cache pattern
- `useArData.ts`: same cache pattern + emits `AR_DATA_LOADED` from cache immediately

**Commit `c1e85d0`** — "fix: remove unused MAX_CARDS and cardCount after PiP removal (TS6133)"
- `ARContainerV2.tsx`: removed unused `MAX_CARDS` constant, renamed `cardCount = 1` → `cardCount: _cardCount` in destructuring

### 🔴 Currently Broken — Needs Fix

**Black screen on `/learn-ar` after deployment** — not yet diagnosed or fixed. TypeScript passes (`npx tsc --noEmit` = 0 errors). The issue is a runtime/build problem.

## Next Steps (What to Do)

1. **Read `LearnARV2.tsx` lines 121–400** to find where `ARContainerV2` is rendered and whether there's a conditional (`isLoading`, `appState`, etc.) that could prevent it showing

2. **Read full `ARContainerV2.tsx`** current state to confirm JSX structure is intact (especially the `mainSrc` computation and iframe render condition)

3. **Check the Vercel build** — run `npm run build` locally in `frontend-web/` to see if terser produces any warnings or errors that could indicate bundle corruption

4. **Likely fix candidates** (apply whichever is confirmed):
   - If `LearnARV2` has a conditional hiding `ARContainerV2`: fix the condition
   - If terser is corrupting something: add `terserOptions.compress.pure_funcs` safelist or downgrade to `minify: 'esbuild'` (safer, but keeps console.logs) or use `drop_console` only on specific files
   - If `useArData` localStorage is causing issues: revert to simpler version without stale-while-revalidate
   - If the empty `if (newPhase === 'VIEWING') {}` block causes issues: remove it

5. **After fix**: run `npx tsc --noEmit` → confirm 0 errors → `git add` changed files → `git commit -m "fix: black screen on learn-ar after PiP removal"` → `git push origin main`

334: function PetChatPopup({ petName, word, onClose }: { petName: string; word: string; onClose: () => void }) {
335:     const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
336:     const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
337:     const [displayed, setDisplayed] = useState('');
338: 
339:     useEffect(() => {
340:         let cancelled = false;
341:         const fetchMessage = async () => {
342:             setLoading(true);
343:             setDisplayed('');
344:             try {
345:                 const prompt = word
346:                     ? `You are ${petName}, a friendly pet. The student just learned '${word}'. Say something fun and encouraging in 1 short sentence.`
347:                     : `You are ${petName}, a friendly learning pet. Say a short, fun encouraging message to a student in 1 sentence.`;

349:                     method: 'POST',
350:                     headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
351:                     body: JSON.stringify({ message: prompt, session_id: 'pet-chat' }),
352:                 });
353:                 if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Chat failed');
354:                 const data = await res.json();
355:                 if (!cancelled) setMessage(data.response || 'Keep learning! You are doing great!');
356:             } catch {
357:                 if (!cancelled) setMessage('You are doing amazing! Keep it up!');
358:             } finally {
359:                 if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);

361:         };
362:         fetchMessage();
363:         return () => { cancelled = true; };
364:     }, [petName, word]);

408:                 </div>

<content>1: /**
2:  * ARContainerV2.tsx
3:  *
4:  * AR container with iframe swapping for MindAR + PiP multi-scanner.
5:  *
6:  * Phase flow:
7:  *   SCANNING  → full-screen ar-scanner.html
8:  *   LOADING   → blank (waiting for mindUrl)
9:  *   VIEWING   → full-screen ar-viewer.html  +  PiP ar-scanner.html (top-left, 120px)
10:  *
11:  * The PiP scanner keeps running during VIEWING so the user can scan a second
12:  * flashcard without leaving the AR experience.
13:  */
14: 
15: import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
16: import { eventBus } from '@/runtime/EventBus';
17: import { AREvent } from '@/core/types/AREvents';
18: import {
19:     ARMessage,
20:     ARMessageType,
21:     ARMessagePayloadMap,
22:     createMessage,
23:     normalizeMessage
24: } from '@/core/types/ARMessages';
25: 
26: // ========== TYPES ==========
27: export type ARPhase = 'IDLE' | 'SCANNING' | 'LOADING' | 'VIEWING' | 'ERROR'
28:     | 'GAME_DRAG' | 'GAME_MEMORY' | 'GAME_COLORING';
29: 
30: interface ARContainerV2Props {
31:     initialPhase?: ARPhase;
32:     mindUrl?: string;
33:     modelUrl?: string;
34:     imageUrl?: string;
35:     modelUrl2?: string;
36:     imageUrl2?: string;
37:     /** Number of flashcards already loaded — kept for parent API compatibility */
38:     cardCount?: number;
39:     onPhaseChange?: (phase: ARPhase) => void;
40:     onQRDetected?: (qrId: string) => void;
41:     onTargetFound?: (targetIndex: number) => void;
42:     onTargetLost?: (targetIndex: number) => void;
43:     onModelClick?: (modelId: string, targetIndex?: number) => void;
44:     onComboDetected?: (targets: number[]) => void;
45:     children?: React.ReactNode;
46: }
47: 
48: // ========== COMPONENT ==========
49: export const ARContainerV2: React.FC<ARContainerV2Props> = ({
50:     initialPhase = 'SCANNING',
51:     mindUrl,
52:     modelUrl,
53:     imageUrl,
54:     modelUrl2,
55:     imageUrl2,
56:     cardCount: _cardCount,   // accepted for API compatibility, not used internally
57:     onPhaseChange,
58:     onQRDetected,
59:     onTargetFound,
60:     onTargetLost,
61:     onModelClick,
62:     onComboDetected,
63:     children
64: }) => {
65:     const [phase, setPhase] = useState<ARPhase>(initialPhase);
66:     const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
67:     const [isReady, setIsReady] = useState(false);
68: 
69:     const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);       // main iframe
70:     const pipRef    = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);       // background scanner iframe (invisible)
71: 
72:     // ========== VIEWER SRC ==========
73:     const getViewerSrc = useCallback(() => {
74:         if (!mindUrl) return null;
75:         const params = new URLSearchParams();
76:         params.set('mind', mindUrl);
77:         if (modelUrl)  params.set('model',  modelUrl);
78:         if (imageUrl)  params.set('image',  imageUrl);
79:         if (modelUrl2) params.set('model2', modelUrl2);
80:         if (imageUrl2) params.set('image2', imageUrl2);
81:         return `/ar-viewer.html?${params.toString()}`;
82:     }, [mindUrl, modelUrl, imageUrl, modelUrl2, imageUrl2]);
83: 
84:     // Main iframe src (only scanner or viewer — not both at once)
85:     const mainSrc = (() => {
86:         switch (phase) {
87:             case 'SCANNING': return '/ar-scanner.html';
88:             case 'VIEWING':  return getViewerSrc();
89:             default:         return null;
90:         }
91:     })();
92: 
93:     // ========== PHASE TRANSITIONS ==========
94:     const transitionTo = useCallback((newPhase: ARPhase) => {
95:         console.log(`[ARContainerV2] Phase: ${phase} → ${newPhase}`);
96:         setPhase(newPhase);
97:         onPhaseChange?.(newPhase);
98:         eventBus.emit('AR_PHASE_CHANGED' as any, { phase: newPhase });
99: 
100:         // When entering VIEWING, background scanner iframe auto-mounts (no state needed)
101:         if (newPhase === 'VIEWING') {
102:             // nothing extra — pipRef iframe mounts automatically when phase === 'VIEWING'
103:         }
104:     }, [phase, onPhaseChange]);
105: 
106:     // ========== SEND TO IFRAME HELPERS ==========
107:     const sendToMain = useCallback((type: string, data: any = {}) => {
108:         iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage({ type, ...data }, '*');
109:     }, []);
110: 
111:     const sendToPiP = useCallback((type: string, data: any = {}) => {
112:         pipRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage({ type, ...data }, '*');
113:     }, []);
114: 
115:     /** Send to whichever scanner is currently active */
116:     const sendToScanner = useCallback((type: string, data: any = {}) => {
117:         if (phase === 'SCANNING') {
118:             sendToMain(type, data);
119:         } else {
120:             sendToPiP(type, data);
121:         }
122:     }, [phase, sendToMain, sendToPiP]);
123: 
124:     // ========== SEND TYPED MESSAGE (New Protocol) ==========
125:     const sendTypedMessage = useCallback(<K extends ARMessageType>(
126:         type: K,
127:         payload: K extends keyof ARMessagePayloadMap ? ARMessagePayloadMap[K] : unknown
128:     ) => {
129:         if (!iframeRef.current?.contentWindow) {
130:             console.warn('[ARBridge] Iframe not ready');
131:             return;
132:         }
133:         if (!isReady && type !== 'INITIAL_STATE') {
134:             console.warn('[ARBridge] Waiting for SYSTEM_READY');
135:             return;
136:         }
137:         const message = createMessage(type, payload);
138:         iframeRef.current.contentWindow.postMessage(message, '*');
139:         console.log('[ARBridge] 📤', type, payload);
140:     }, [isReady]);
141: 
142:     // ========== MESSAGE HANDLING ==========
143:     useEffect(() => {
144:         const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
145:             const msg = normalizeMessage(event.data);
146:             if (!msg) return;
147: 
148:             const { type, payload } = msg as ARMessage;
149: 
150:             // Determine which iframe sent this
151:             const fromPiP = event.source === pipRef.current?.contentWindow;
152:             console.log(`[ARBridge] 📥 ${fromPiP ? '[PiP]' : '[Main]'} ${type}`, payload);
153: 
154:             switch (type) {
155:                 // ── Handshake ──
156:                 case 'SYSTEM_READY':
157:                     if (!fromPiP) {
158:                         setIsReady(true);
159:                         eventBus.emit(AREvent.SCENE_READY, payload as any);
160:                     }
161:                     break;
162: 
163:                 case 'SCANNER_READY':
164:                     if (!fromPiP) {
165:                         setIsReady(true);
166:                         eventBus.emit(AREvent.SCENE_READY, { scene: 'scanner' } as any);
167:                     }
168:                     break;
169: 
170:                 // ── QR detected ──
171:                 case 'QR_DETECTED': {
172:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['QR_DETECTED'];
173:                     console.log(`[ARBridge] 🎯 QR Detected (${fromPiP ? 'PiP' : 'Main'}):`, data.qrId);
174:                     onQRDetected?.(data.qrId);
175:                     eventBus.emit(AREvent.MARKER_FOUND, { markerId: data.qrId, target: null } as any);
176: 
177:                     if (!fromPiP && phase === 'SCANNING') {
178:                         // First scan from the main scanner → transition to viewer
179:                         transitionTo('LOADING');
180:                     }
181:                     // If fromPiP (second scan during VIEWING) → just notify parent via
182:                     // onQRDetected above; parent will update modelUrl2/imageUrl2 props.
183:                     break;
184:                 }
185: 
186:                 // ── Scanner error ──
187:                 case 'SCANNER_ERROR': {
188:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['SCANNER_ERROR'];
189:                     if (!fromPiP) {
190:                         // Fatal only when the main scanner has the error
191:                         setError(data.error);
192:                         transitionTo('ERROR');
193:                     } else {
194:                         // Background scanner error is non-fatal — log and ignore
195:                         console.warn('[ARBridge] Background scanner error (non-fatal):', data.error);
196:                     }
197:                     break;
198:                 }
199: 
200:                 // ── Viewer events ──
201:                 case 'AR_READY':
202:                     setIsReady(true);
203:                     transitionTo('VIEWING');
204:                     eventBus.emit(AREvent.SCENE_READY, { scene: 'viewer' } as any);
205:                     break;
206: 
207:                 case 'TARGET_FOUND': {
208:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['TARGET_FOUND'];
209:                     onTargetFound?.(data.targetIndex);
210:                     eventBus.emit(AREvent.MARKER_FOUND, {
211:                         markerId: `target-${data.targetIndex}`,
212:                         target: null
213:                     } as any);
214:                     break;
215:                 }
216: 
217:                 case 'TARGET_LOST': {
218:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['TARGET_LOST'];
219:                     onTargetLost?.(data.targetIndex);
220:                     eventBus.emit(AREvent.MARKER_LOST, { markerId: `target-${data.targetIndex}` } as any);
221:                     break;
222:                 }
223: 
224:                 case 'MULTI_TARGET_DETECTED': {
225:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['MULTI_TARGET_DETECTED'];
226:                     eventBus.emit('MULTI_TARGET_DETECTED' as any, data);
227:                     break;
228:                 }
229: 
230:                 case 'MODEL_CLICKED': {
231:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['MODEL_CLICKED'] & { targetIndex?: number };
232:                     onModelClick?.(data.modelId, data.targetIndex);
233:                     eventBus.emit('AR_MODEL_CLICKED' as any, { modelId: data.modelId, targetIndex: data.targetIndex });
234:                     break;
235:                 }
236: 
237:                 case 'COMBO_DETECTED': {
238:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['COMBO_DETECTED'];
239:                     onComboDetected?.(data.targets);
240:                     eventBus.emit(AREvent.COMBO_ACTIVATED, {
241:                         tag1: `target-${data.targets[0]}`,
242:                         tag2: `target-${data.targets[1]}`
243:                     } as any);
244:                     break;
245:                 }
246: 
247:                 case 'ANIMATION_COMPLETE': {
248:                     const data = payload as ARMessagePayloadMap['ANIMATION_COMPLETE'];
249:                     eventBus.emit('ANIMATION_COMPLETE' as any, data);
250:                     break;
251:                 }
252: 
253:                 case 'SYSTEM_ERROR':
254:                 case 'AR_ERROR' as any: {
255:                     const data = payload as { error?: string; message?: string };
256:                     const errorMsg = data.error || data.message || 'Unknown error';
257:                     setError(errorMsg);
258:                     eventBus.emit(AREvent.AR_ERROR, { error: new Error(errorMsg) } as any);
259:                     break;
260:                 }
261:             }
262:         };
263: 
264:         window.addEventListener('message', handleMessage);
265:         return () => window.removeEventListener('message', handleMessage);
266:     }, [phase, onQRDetected, onTargetFound, onTargetLost, onModelClick, onComboDetected, transitionTo]);
267: 
268:     // ========== EXTERNAL CONTROLS (EventBus) ==========
269:     useEffect(() => {
270:         const handleSwitchToViewer = (data: any) => {
271:             console.log('[ARContainerV2] Switching to viewer:', data.mindUrl);
272:             setTimeout(() => transitionTo('VIEWING'), 100);
273:         };
274:         const handleSwitchToScanner = () => transitionTo('SCANNING');
275:         const handleSetMode = (data: any) => sendToMain('SET_MODE', { mode: data.mode });
276:         const handleARCommand = (data: { type: ARMessageType; payload: any }) => {
277:             sendTypedMessage(data.type, data.payload);
278:         };
279:         const handleResumeScan = () => {
280:             sendToScanner('RESUME_SCANNING');
281:         };
282: 
283:         eventBus.on('AR_SWITCH_TO_VIEWER'  as any, handleSwitchToViewer);
284:         eventBus.on('AR_SWITCH_TO_SCANNER' as any, handleSwitchToScanner);
285:         eventBus.on('AR_SET_MODE'          as any, handleSetMode);
286:         eventBus.on('AR_COMMAND'           as any, handleARCommand);
287:         eventBus.on('AR_RESUME_SCAN'       as any, handleResumeScan);
288: 
289:         return () => {
290:             eventBus.off('AR_SWITCH_TO_VIEWER'  as any, handleSwitchToViewer);
291:             eventBus.off('AR_SWITCH_TO_SCANNER' as any, handleSwitchToScanner);
292:             eventBus.off('AR_SET_MODE'          as any, handleSetMode);
293:             eventBus.off('AR_COMMAND'           as any, handleARCommand);
294:             eventBus.off('AR_RESUME_SCAN'       as any, handleResumeScan);
295:         };
296:     }, [transitionTo, sendToMain, sendToScanner, sendTypedMessage, phase]);
297: 
298:     // ========== AUTO TRANSITION LOADING → VIEWING ==========
299:     useEffect(() => {
300:         if (phase === 'LOADING' && mindUrl) {
301:             console.log('[ARContainerV2] mindUrl ready → VIEWING');
302:             const t = setTimeout(() => transitionTo('VIEWING'), 100);
303:             return () => clearTimeout(t);
304:         }
305:     }, [phase, mindUrl, transitionTo]);
306: 
307:     return (
308:         <div
309:             className="ar-container-v2"
310:             style={{
311:                 position: 'fixed',
312:                 inset: 0,
313:                 width: '100vw',
314:                 height: '100vh',
315:                 // @ts-ignore - dvh for mobile
316:                 height: '100dvh',
317:                 background: '#000',
318:                 overflow: 'hidden',
319:                 zIndex: 99999,
320:             }}
321:         >
322:             {/* ── Error state ── */}
323:             {phase === 'ERROR' && (

328:                         background: '#000',

342:                     </svg>
343:                     <p style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>{error || 'An error occurred'}</p>
344:                     <button
345:                         onClick={() => transitionTo('SCANNING')}
346:                         style={{

349:                             background: '#4ECDC4',
350:                             border: 'none',
351:                             borderRadius: 20,
352:                             color: '#fff',

356:                         }}
357:                     >

364:             {mainSrc && (

13: function getCached<T>(key: string): T | null {
14:   try {
15:     const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
16:     if (!raw) return null;
17:     const { data, ts } = JSON.parse(raw) as { data: T; ts: number };

19:     return data;
20:   } catch { return null; }

52: export const useArData = (qrId: string | null) => {

64:       if (!path) return undefined;
65:       if (path.startsWith('http://') || path.startsWith('https://')) return path;
66:       const cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
67:       return `${API_BASE}${cleanPath}`;
68:     };
69:

71:       typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : '';
72: 
73:     const transformRaw = (data: any): ArData => {

## Assistant (Build · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 11.1s)

The build succeeds. Now I can identify the issues clearly. Let me analyze what I found:

**Finding 1 — Duplicate `height` key warning in `ARContainerV2.tsx` (line 314–316):**
```tsx
height: '100vh',
// @ts-ignore - dvh for mobile
height: '100dvh',
```
This is a duplicate key in an object literal. While it compiles, **terser likely strips the first occurrence**, leaving only `100dvh`. On iOS Safari, `100dvh` is supported in iOS 15.4+ but this is not the issue causing black screen.

**Finding 2 — The real problem: `drop_console: true`**  
The build strips ALL `console.log` calls. But crucially, `console.warn` in `sendTypedMessage` (line 131–134 of ARContainerV2.tsx) uses `console.warn`, not `console.log`. That's fine.

## Assistant (Build · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 26.1s)

I now have a clear picture of the problem. Here's my analysis:

**Root cause identified: `drop_console: true` kills the debug output from the mobile panel, but the actual black screen is caused by a different issue.**

Looking at `ARContainerV2.tsx` lines 307–319:
```tsx
style={{
    position: 'fixed',
    inset: 0,
    width: '100vw',
    height: '100vh',
    // @ts-ignore - dvh for mobile
    height: '100dvh',     ← DUPLICATE KEY
    background: '#000',
    ...
    zIndex: 99999,
}}
```

The **duplicate `height` key** means JavaScript object literal only keeps the **last** value. So the container gets `height: '100dvh'`. That's probably fine on modern iOS.

**The real black screen cause**: `drop_console: true` also drops `console.warn` and `console.error`? No — terser's `drop_console: true` drops **all** `console.*` calls including `console.warn`. This means:

In `sendTypedMessage` (ARContainerV2.tsx line 131–134):
```tsx
if (!iframeRef.current?.contentWindow) {
    console.warn('[ARBridge] Iframe not ready');  ← DROPPED by terser
    return;   ← BUT THE RETURN IS STILL THERE
}
```

The `return` statement itself is NOT dropped — only the `console.warn` call is removed. So the logic is intact.

**Actually**, after careful re-reading: `drop_console: true` in terser removes the **entire console call statement** but keeps the surrounding `if` block and `return`. So logic is preserved.

The real culprit I see: **The background PiP iframe is missing the `allow="camera"` attribute for iOS**. When `phase === 'VIEWING'`, two iframes both want camera access. iOS Safari only grants camera to one iframe at a time, and with the PiP being mounted simultaneously, it may be blocking the main scanner iframe from ever getting camera during the initial SCANNING phase.

Wait — let me re-read the flow. During `SCANNING` phase:
- Only the main iframe (`ar-scanner.html`) renders — PiP is NOT mounted (only mounts during `VIEWING`)
- So camera conflict is NOT the issue during SCANNING

**The actual issue I now see**: The mobile debug log says "completely black, only 3 log lines (debug panel init) then nothing" — this means React's `console.log` calls are absent because `drop_console:true` stripped them. The REAL question is whether the IFRAME renders and the camera appears.

Let me re-examine: The build warning says:
```
[plugin vite:esbuild] src/components/ar/ARContainerV2.tsx: Duplicate key "height" in object literal
```

This is an **esbuild warning** (not terser). The duplicate key is silently resolved by keeping the last one. Not a crash.

**The most likely actual cause**: After commit `c1e85d0`, the `ARContainerV2` removed `showPiP`/`pipExpanded` state. But looking at the current code — `ARContainerV2.tsx` lines 307–410 — the JSX structure looks correct. The main iframe renders for `SCANNING` phase.

5: (function () {
6:     'use strict';
7: 
8:     const video = document.getElementById('video');
9:     const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');
10:     const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
11: 
12:     let scanning = true;
13:     let lastDetectedCode = null;
14:     let detectionCooldown = false;
15: 
16:     // ============ CAMERA INIT ============
17:     async function initCamera() {
18:         try {
19:             const constraints = {
20:                 video: {
21:                     facingMode: 'environment',
22:                     width: { ideal: 1280 },
23:                     height: { ideal: 720 }
24:                 },
25:                 audio: false
26:             };
27: 
28:             const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia(constraints);
29:             video.srcObject = stream;
30: 
31:             video.onloadedmetadata = () => {
32:                 canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
33:                 canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
34:                 log('📷', `Camera ready: ${video.videoWidth}x${video.videoHeight}`);
35:                 startScanning();
36: 
37:                 sendToParent('SCANNER_READY', {
38:                     width: video.videoWidth,
39:                     height: video.videoHeight
40:                 });
41:             };
42:         } catch (err) {
43:             log('❌', 'Camera error: ' + err.message);
44:             sendToParent('SCANNER_ERROR', { error: err.message });
45:         }
46:     }
47: 
48:     // ============ QR SCANNING ============
49:     function startScanning() {
50:         setInterval(scanFrame, 200);
51:     }
52: 
53:     function scanFrame() {
54:         if (!scanning || video.readyState !== video.HAVE_ENOUGH_DATA) return;
55: 
56:         ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
57:         const imageData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
58: 
59:         const code = jsQR(imageData.data, imageData.width, imageData.height, {
60:             inversionAttempts: 'dontInvert'
61:         });
62: 
63:         if (code && code.data) {
64:             handleDetection(code.data);
65:         }
66:     }
67: 
68:     function handleDetection(data) {
69:         if (detectionCooldown || data === lastDetectedCode) return;
70: 
71:         lastDetectedCode = data;

## Assistant (Build · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 34.1s)

Now I see the **critical bug**. The `ar-scanner.js` posts a **legacy format** message:
```js
window.parent.postMessage({ type, ...data }, '*');
// → { type: 'SCANNER_READY', width: 1280, height: 720 }
```

The `normalizeMessage` function in `ARMessages.ts` handles legacy messages via `isLegacyMessage`:
```ts
// This checks: has 'type' AND does NOT have 'origin'
// { type: 'SCANNER_READY', width: 1280, height: 720 } ← no 'origin' → passes isLegacyMessage
```

So `SCANNER_READY` is correctly normalized. That part is fine.

**Now the actual black screen problem**: Looking at the `QR_DETECTED` case in `ARContainerV2.tsx` line 171–183:

When a QR is detected during SCANNING (not fromPiP), it calls `transitionTo('LOADING')`. Then `ARContainerV2`'s auto-transition effect (lines 299–305):
```tsx
useEffect(() => {
    if (phase === 'LOADING' && mindUrl) {
        const t = setTimeout(() => transitionTo('VIEWING'), 100);
        return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }
}, [phase, mindUrl, transitionTo]);
```

But this is in `ARContainerV2`. `mindUrl` is passed as a prop from `LearnARV2`. When the QR is detected, `LearnARV2.handleQRDetected` sets `detectedQrId` and calls `addFlashcard`. Then `useArData(detectedQrId)` starts fetching — but `mindUrl` won't be available until fetch completes (cold start = 70s on Render). So `ARContainerV2` stays in `LOADING` phase with `mainSrc = null` (black screen) until `mindUrl` prop arrives.

**That's expected behavior**. The issue is different during SCANNING — the scanner iframe should show the camera.

Let me focus on what actually changed that could cause the INITIAL black screen where SCANNER_READY never fires:

The build warning from esbuild about the **duplicate `height` key** is benign. But let me think about what terser does to the `ARContainerV2` code with `drop_console: true`.

Looking at `transitionTo` (line 94–104):
```tsx
const transitionTo = useCallback((newPhase: ARPhase) => {
    console.log(`[ARContainerV2] Phase: ${phase} → ${newPhase}`);  ← DROPPED
    setPhase(newPhase);
    onPhaseChange?.(newPhase);
    eventBus.emit('AR_PHASE_CHANGED' as any, { phase: newPhase });

    if (newPhase === 'VIEWING') {
        // nothing extra
    }
}, [phase, onPhaseChange]);
```

After terser drops the console.log, this becomes:
```js
const transitionTo = useCallback((newPhase) => {
    setPhase(newPhase);
    onPhaseChange?.(newPhase);
    eventBus.emit('AR_PHASE_CHANGED', { phase: newPhase });
    if (newPhase === 'VIEWING') {}
}, [phase, onPhaseChange]);
```

The empty `if` block is harmless.

**Real issue I think I've been missing**: The problem might actually be **iOS camera permissions inside an iframe**. When we changed from showing the scanner directly to using an iframe, iOS Safari requires the **parent page** to have `camera` in the `allow` attribute of the iframe.

Looking at `ARContainerV2.tsx` line 369:
```tsx
allow="camera; microphone; autoplay; fullscreen"
```

That's correct. The main iframe has camera permission.

Let me look at one more thing — **was there something in the previous working version of `ARContainerV2.tsx`** that's now missing? The `drop_console: true` removes ALL console.* calls. In `sendTypedMessage`, there's:

```tsx
if (!iframeRef.current?.contentWindow) {
    console.warn('[ARBridge] Iframe not ready');
    return;
}
```

After terser, `drop_console: true` also drops `console.warn`. So this becomes:
```js
if (!iframeRef.current?.contentWindow) {
    return;
}
```

This is fine — the return is still there.

## Assistant (Build · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 16.1s)

**Critical finding!** This is the smoking gun.

The `mobile-debug.js` **overrides `console.log`/`console.warn`/`console.error`** at lines 182–200. But this happens BEFORE React hydrates (it's in `index.html` as a plain `<script>`).

With `drop_console: true` in terser, **ALL console.* calls are stripped from the React bundle**. So when `mobile-debug.js` overrides `console.log`, there's nothing to intercept — the React app has no `console.*` calls at all.

**But the real problem**: The debug panel's `window.onerror` handler (line 203) and `window.onunhandledrejection` handler (line 208) should have caught any React crash and shown it in the panel. Since "nothing" appeared after the 3 init logs, it means **React didn't crash** — it's running but producing no visible output.

The `ar-scanner.js` itself has `console.log` calls (e.g., `log('📷', ...)`) that are NOT terser-processed (it's in `/public`, not in the React bundle). So those would appear in the debug panel if the scanner iframe loaded and camera worked.

**BUT**: The debug panel is in the **parent window**. The scanner iframe's `console.log` calls go to the **iframe's console**, NOT the parent window's console. So the debug panel would never see logs from the scanner iframe anyway.

So the "3 lines then nothing" is perfectly consistent with a working React app that has `drop_console:true` — no React logs, and no iframe logs visible in parent. 

**This means the black screen might actually be the camera not activating in the iframe** — and we just can't see it because:
1. Terser stripped all React console logs (so no React-side debug output)
2. Scanner iframe logs go to iframe's own console (invisible to parent debug panel)

**The real question is: Does the scanner iframe even show video?**

Here's my hypothesis for the actual bug: When we check the `index.html`:

## Assistant (Build · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 26.5s)

Now I have the complete picture. Let me reason through this systematically:

**What the debug session shows:**
- Session 2 (new build, black screen): 3 lines — "Mobile Debug Panel Ready", "User Agent: ...", "URL: ..."
- Then nothing

**What this means:**
- React mounts and renders `LearnARV2` → `ARContainerV2` 
- The main iframe (`ar-scanner.html`) is injected into the DOM
- `ar-scanner.html` loads jsQR from CDN and runs `ar-scanner.js`
- `ar-scanner.js` calls `initCamera()` → `getUserMedia()`
- The camera video renders in the iframe

**The black screen is the parent page background = `background: '#000'`** in `ARContainerV2`. If the iframe is rendering on top of it with the camera, the user would see the camera. If the iframe itself shows black, something is wrong in the scanner.

**Wait** — I just realized: the debug session says "black screen" with "only 3 log lines". But the scanner runs in an iframe. The scanner's `log()` function does `console.log('[AR-Scanner] ...)` which goes to the **iframe's console**. The mobile debug panel only captures logs from the **parent window**. So even if the camera is working perfectly in the iframe, we'd see no scanner logs in the debug panel.

**BUT** — `ar-scanner.js` calls `sendToParent('SCANNER_READY', ...)` which does `window.parent.postMessage(...)`. The `ARContainerV2` in the parent listens for `message` events and calls `console.log('[ARBridge] 📥 ...')`. With `drop_console: true`, that `console.log` is **gone**. So we'd see no `[ARBridge]` logs even when `SCANNER_READY` fires correctly.

**This means the "black screen" description and "no hook activity" in the logs is actually expected behavior with `drop_console: true`.**

The user may be seeing a working AR scanner but misinterpreting the lack of logs as "nothing is happening." OR... the iframe is genuinely not showing camera.

Actually, the better fix is: **add a visible non-console UI indicator in `ar-scanner.html`** that shows camera status without relying on console.log. OR — since `drop_console: true` is the visibility problem — we need to change the debug strategy.

**But the actual question is whether the camera is working**. Let me think about what could actually cause a black screen (no camera video) after our commits:

1. The iframe renders correctly ✓ (ARContainerV2 JSX is intact)
2. `ar-scanner.html` loads ✓ (it's a static public file, unchanged)  
3. `ar-scanner.js` runs and calls `getUserMedia` 
4. `getUserMedia` might fail on iOS Safari if:
   - Camera permission not granted (user never saw the prompt)
   - The page uses `http://` instead of `https://` (getUserMedia requires secure context)
   - The iframe doesn't have proper `allow="camera"` attribute

Wait — **there is a subtle iOS Safari issue with camera in iframes**: On iOS, camera permission for an iframe must be explicitly granted via the `allow` attribute AND the parent page must also have camera permissions. Both the main iframe and the PiP iframe need `allow="camera"`. Both have it in the current code.

Actually, the cleanest approach: patch `mobile-debug.js` to listen for `message` events on the parent window, which will catch all `postMessage` calls from the scanner iframe. This gives full visibility into whether `SCANNER_READY` fires, even with `drop_console: true` stripping React's `console.log` calls.

**Tool: edit**

**Input:**
```json
{
  "filePath": "E:\\Daniel Project\\Graduted Project\\Edu-platform\\frontend-web\\public\\static\\ar-assets\\js\\mobile-debug.js",
  "oldString": "    // Initial log\n    console.log('🔧 Mobile Debug Panel Ready');\n    console.log('📱 User Agent:', navigator.userAgent);\n    console.log('🌐 URL:', window.location.href);\n\n})();",
  "newString": "    // ========== INTERCEPT postMessage FROM IFRAMES ==========\n    // The React bundle uses drop_console:true in production, stripping all\n    // console.* calls. But iframe→parent postMessage events still fire.\n    // We intercept them here so the debug panel shows AR iframe communication\n    // even when React's own logging is absent.\n    const originalAddEventListener = window.addEventListener.bind(window);\n    window.addEventListener('message', function (event) {\n        try {\n            const data = event.data;\n            if (data && typeof data === 'object' && data.type) {\n                const typeStr = String(data.type);\n                // Only show AR-related messages to avoid noise\n                const arTypes = [\n                    'SCANNER_READY', 'QR_DETECTED', 'SCANNER_ERROR',\n                    'AR_READY', 'TARGET_FOUND', 'TARGET_LOST',\n                    'SYSTEM_READY', 'SYSTEM_ERROR', 'AR_ERROR',\n                    'MULTI_TARGET_DETECTED', 'MODEL_CLICKED',\n                ];\n                if (arTypes.includes(typeStr)) {\n                    const payload = JSON.stringify(data, null, 1);\n                    addLog('info', [`📨 [iframe→parent] ${typeStr}`, payload]);\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (_e) { /* ignore parse errors */ }\n    });\n\n    // Initial log\n    console.log('🔧 Mobile Debug Panel Ready');\n    console.log('📱 User Agent:', navigator.userAgent);\n    console.log('🌐 URL:', window.location.href);\n\n})();"
}

## Assistant (Build · claude-sonnet-4.6 · 108.7s)

Pushed as commit `59f5397`. Here's a summary of everything done and the current state:

---

## What was done this session

### Code investigation findings

After reading the full source, the React component structure (`ARContainerV2`, `LearnARV2`) is **structurally intact** — the JSX renders correctly, iframe has `allow="camera"`, and `normalizeMessage` handles the legacy postMessage format from the scanner. The build completes cleanly (`tsc --noEmit` = 0 errors).

### Root cause of "no logs" diagnosed

The "black screen + only 3 debug lines" from Session 2 was **misread as a React crash**, but is actually expected behavior with `drop_console: true`:

- React's 200+ `console.*` calls are gone from the production bundle → debug panel shows nothing from React
- The scanner iframe's `console.log` calls go to the **iframe's own console**, not the parent window → debug panel never sees them either
- The 3 lines seen ("Mobile Debug Panel Ready" etc.) are from `mobile-debug.js` which runs before terser kicks in (it's a plain `/public` file)

**The camera may actually be working** but we have zero instrumentation to confirm it.

### What was committed (`59f5397`)

Three public files patched (not processed by terser — safe):

1. **`mobile-debug.js`** — Added `window.addEventListener('message', ...)` interceptor. Now when the scanner iframe posts `SCANNER_READY`, `QR_DETECTED`, `SCANNER_ERROR` etc., those messages appear in the debug panel as blue `[iframe→parent]` entries — completely bypassing `drop_console`.

2. **`ar-scanner.html`** — Added `#scanner-status` div (visible only with `?debug=true`) showing camera state inline in the iframe viewport.

3. **`ar-scanner.js`** — Updates the status overlay through init stages: `⏳ Requesting camera...` → `📸 Camera granted, waiting for video...` → `✅ Camera ready - scan a QR code` (then hides after 3s).

### Next step for you

Open `/learn-ar?debug=true` on the iPhone and look at the debug panel:

- **If `📨 [iframe→parent] SCANNER_READY` appears** → React is working fine; the "black screen" was just missing camera video (possible camera permission issue on that device)
- **If `📨 [iframe→parent] SCANNER_ERROR` appears** → The error text will tell you exactly what iOS Safari rejected (e.g. `"NotAllowedError"` = camera denied, `"NotFoundError"` = no back camera)
- **If `⏳ Requesting camera...` appears in the iframe** but then nothing → `getUserMedia` is hanging (iOS Safari waiting for user prompt that's not showing)
- **If nothing from iframe appears** → The iframe itself isn't loading (network/CSP issue)
